# Bibliography

External literature here is **signal**, not sovereignty. Full pre-compaction citations, URLs, and annotations are retained in `HOT-SURFACE-COMPACTION-ORIGINALS.json`.

## Hot-surface compaction note

rev0337 compacts this hot bibliography surface to REF ids plus contract-bearing URL/load-bearing-use fields. Full citation title lines, URLs, and annotations are retained losslessly in `HOT-SURFACE-COMPACTION-ORIGINALS.json` and verified by `tools/check_hot_surface_compaction_contract.py`.
This compact surface is a locator index, not a citation-substitute, bibliography court, or evidence-erasure permission.

- `REF-1091` — Nelson F. Liu et al., “Lost in the Middle: How Language Models Use Long Contexts,” arXiv:2307.03172.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.03172
  - Load-bearing use: supports the rev0360 claim that full-context availability alone does not prove reliable use of middle-position evidence.

- `REF-1092` — “NoLiMa: Long-Context Evaluation Beyond Literal Matching,” arXiv:2502.05167.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05167
  - Load-bearing use: supports non-literal and decoy challenge slices instead of literal retrieval-only archive tests.

- `REF-1093` — “Positional Failures in Long-Context LLMs,” arXiv:2605.23170.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23170
  - Load-bearing use: supports position-rotated smoke-slice evaluation for DelayBasin reentry packets.

- `REF-1094` — “MemoryAgentBench,” arXiv:2507.05257.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05257
  - Load-bearing use: supports testing retrieval, test-time learning, long-range understanding, and conflict handling as distinct memory competencies.

- `REF-1095` — “MemConflict,” arXiv:2605.20926.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20926
  - Load-bearing use: supports conflict-bearing memory tests and warns against answer-only memory evaluation.

- `REF-1096` — “EverMemBench,” arXiv:2602.01313.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01313
  - Load-bearing use: supports temporal and multi-hop memory stress tests for long-horizon continuation.

- `REF-1097` — “Search-Time Data Contamination,” arXiv:2508.13180.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13180
  - Load-bearing use: supports avoiding public static answer leakage in search-augmented or archive-augmented assays.

- `REF-1098` — “Prompt-Level Distillation,” arXiv:2602.21103.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21103
  - Load-bearing use: supports the speculation that a public prompt/archive packet can act like a transparent natural-language continuation program.

- `REF-1099` — “On-Policy Context Distillation,” arXiv:2602.12275.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12275
  - Load-bearing use: provides contrast for DelayBasin's public external-context approach versus compressing context into model weights.



- `REF-1100` — Liu et al., “A Scalable Benchmark for Repository-Oriented Long-Horizon Conversational Context Management (LoCoEval),” arXiv:2603.06358.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06358
  - Load-bearing use: supports treating repository artifacts plus conversation state as a distinct long-horizon context-management challenge for the smoke-slice fixture.

- `REF-1101` — “Governing Evolving Memory in LLM Agents,” arXiv:2603.11768.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11768
  - Load-bearing use: supports including poisoning, drift, conflict, and hallucination risks in sham/no-archive negative canaries.

- `REF-1102` — “Don't Ask the LLM to Track Freshness: A Deterministic Recipe for Memory Conflict Resolution,” arXiv:2606.01435.
  - URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01435
  - Load-bearing use: supports using deterministic post-retrieval fixtures and explicit score aggregation instead of informal LLM judgment for memory-conflict slices.
